<div class="views-field views-field-views-conditional"> <span class="field-content"><span class="proCat proCat-7">Partners</span>
<a href="/our-stories/un-volunteers-enhance-food-security-and-sustainability-crisis-affected-people-nigeria"><img alt="UN Volunteer Tiruneh Debena after validation of the beneficiary targeting and monitoring the distribution of food inputs to conflict-affected communities in Yola, Adamawa State, North-East Nigeria." class="img-responsive" height="240" src="https://www.unv.org/sites/default/files/styles/related_content/public/Nigeria_Beneficiary_validation_and_distribution_monitoring_for_livestock_programme_Yola_North_Adamawa_state_web2.jpg?itok=O_nqZtLw" title="UN Volunteer Tiruneh Debena after validation of the beneficiary targeting and monitoring the distribution of food inputs to conflict-affected communities in Yola, Adamawa State, North-East Nigeria." width="360"/></a>
<div class="conregion"><a href="/search?search_api_views_fulltext=&amp;field_un_region%5B%5D=4"><span>Sub-Saharan Africa </span></a><a href="/search?search_api_views_fulltext=&amp;field_sustainable_development_go%5B%5D=78">SDG 2: Zero hunger</a>, <a href="/search?search_api_views_fulltext=&amp;field_sustainable_development_go%5B%5D=60">SDG 17: Partnerships for the goals</a> </div>

<div class="panel-separator"></div><div class="panel-pane pane-entity-field pane-paragraphs-item-field-campaining-title">
<div class="pane-content">
<div class="field field-name-field-campaining-title field-type-text field-label-hidden"><div class="field-items"><div class="field-item even">ARE YOU READY TO BECOME A UN VOLUNTEER?</div></div></div> </div>
</div>
<div class="panel-separator"></div><div class="panel-pane pane-entity-field pane-paragraphs-item-field-description-text">
<div class="pane-content">
<div class="field field-name-field-description-text field-type-text-long field-label-hidden"><div class="field-items"><div class="field-item even">You can contribute your time, skills and knowledge through volunteering with the UN. This is an opportunity to create positive impact and being a significant force for achieving peace and development. Make a difference to the lives of many!</div></div></div> </div>
</div>
